# Authoritative manuscript numbers

Generated from frozen outputs. No manuscript text has been edited. Exact values are IEEE double-precision representations read from saved CSV/JSON. Publication rounding is three significant digits. Conventional and robust intervals/P are distinct; beta units vary by endpoint. The machine-readable companion is manuscript_numbers.csv.
